Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the nonlinear object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 889. The moduli space has dimension at most 770 with 3 connected components.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the nonlinear decomposition. By minimality, the element a^889 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 770-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 218.1001 (Convergence Analysis)
